# Scryfall rulings coverage

Generated by `cargo run -p mtg-tools -- rulings-coverage --write docs/RULINGS_COVERAGE.md`. Tests cite rulings with `ruling!("Card Name", "distinctive text")`, which fails if no such ruling exists. Rulings with no engine-testable content are listed with a reason in `docs/rulings-exemptions/*.tsv`. Coverage is counted per ruling text, over the texts in scope: those of playable cards the project hasn't put out of scope (docs/DEFERRED.md: Contraptions, host/augment); a text every card of which is deferred (digital-only cards, sticker sheets and the cards deferred with them, cards legal in no format, the dexterity cards, the Conspiracy face-down draft cards) is deferred rather than open. `python3 scripts/rulings_batches.py summary` counts the same way and prints the same `rulings:` line; `cargo test -p mtg-tools` keeps the two in step.

- Total rulings: 78948 (34000 unique texts across 19938 cards; 45 texts excluded as found only on Contraptions, host/augment cards, tokens or emblems)
- Ruling texts in scope: **33955**
- (card, ruling) pairs cited by tests: **26775**
- Ruling texts covered by tests: **26013** (76.61%)
- Ruling texts exempt: **186** (0.55%)
- Ruling texts deferred: **1864**
- Ruling texts open: **5892**
- `rulings: texts=33955 cited=26013 exempt=186 open=5892 deferred=1864 excluded=45`
